# Identifier-hit inventory

Audited HEAD: `6c21d8b1f97ac8175eba02bb9d1b4eb901571f34`. Case-insensitive pattern: `uncloud|uncld|psviderski|ucind|ucnet`. 859 distinct matching blobs, 193 historical paths. Each path is listed below; every matching blob and its matching line numbers follow. These are search hits, **not** a claim that every file was copied.

“HEAD” means the exact path at the pinned HEAD; renamed survivors are separately visible under their current paths. A path absent from HEAD can still survive on another exported branch. “Rewrite in place” is a content-level recommendation, never permission to remove the whole source file. See [the audit](../uncloud-history-audit.md) for history vs current-tree treatment.
